```
1→"""Queue ranking and plan-aware finalization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, WorkQueueResult
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
     7→    enrich_plan_metadata,
     8→    separate_skipped,
     9→    stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    10→    stamp_positions,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import new_item_ids as _new_item_ids
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    14→    enrich_with_impact,
    15→    group_queue_items,
    16→    item_explain,
    17→    item_sort_key,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    21→
    22→
    23→def finalize_queue(
    24→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    25→    *,
    26→    state: StateModel,
    27→    plan: dict | None,
    28→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    29→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    30→    """Apply ranking, plan ordering, limits, and explain metadata."""
    31→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    32→
    33→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
    34→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    35→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
    36→
    37→    total = len(items)
    38→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
    39→        items = items[:opts.count]
    40→    if opts.explain:
    41→        for item in items:
    42→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
    43→
    44→    return {
    45→        "items": items,
    46→        "total": total,
    47→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
    48→        "new_ids": new_ids,
    49→    }
    50→
    51→
    52→def _plan_presort(
    53→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    54→    state: StateModel,
    55→    plan: dict | None,
    56→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    57→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting."""
    58→    if not plan:
    59→        return set(), []
    60→
    61→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
    62→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
    63→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
    64→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
    65→    items[:] = remaining
    66→    return new_ids, skipped
    67→
    68→
    69→def _plan_postsort(
    70→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    71→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
    72→    plan: dict | None,
    73→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions."""
    76→    if not plan:
    77→        return
    78→    if opts.include_skipped:
    79→        items.extend(skipped)
    80→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = ["finalize_queue"]
    84→
```

```
1→"""Queue input resolution and non-open item gathering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ALL_STATUSES, scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import build_subjective_items
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     9→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    10→
    11→
    12→def resolve_queue_inputs(
    13→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    14→    state: StateModel,
    15→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
    16→    """Resolve plan, scan path, status, and subjective threshold."""
    17→    ctx = opts.context
    18→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
    19→
    20→    scan_path: str | None = (
    21→        state.get("scan_path")
    22→        if opts.scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState"
    23→        else opts.scan_path
    24→    )
    25→
    26→    status = opts.status
    27→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    28→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    29→
    30→    try:
    31→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
    32→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    33→        threshold = 100.0
    34→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
    35→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
    36→
    37→
    38→def gather_subjective_items(
    39→    state: StateModel,
    40→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    41→    threshold: float,
    42→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    43→    """Build subjective dimension candidates for non-open issue list views."""
    44→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    45→        return []
    46→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
    47→        return []
    48→    if opts.chronic:
    49→        return []
    50→
    51→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
    52→        state,
    53→        state.get("issues", {}),
    54→        threshold=threshold,
    55→    )
    56→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
    57→
    58→
    59→__all__ = ["gather_subjective_items", "resolve_queue_inputs"]
    60→
```

```
1→"""Queue item selection from canonical snapshot or raw issue lists."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import gather_subjective_items
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, QueueVisibility
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import build_queue_snapshot
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def select_queue_items(
    15→    state: StateModel,
    16→    *,
    17→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    18→    plan: dict | None,
    19→    scan_path: str | None,
    20→    status: str,
    21→    threshold: float,
    22→    visibility: str,
    23→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    24→    """Select the raw queue item list before ranking/finalization."""
    25→    if status == "open" and not opts.chronic:
    26→        snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=opts,
    29→            plan=plan,
    30→            target_strict=threshold,
    31→        )
    32→        return filter_snapshot_items(
    33→            items_for_visibility(snapshot=snapshot, visibility=visibility),
    34→            opts,
    35→        )
    36→
    37→    items = build_issue_items(
    38→        state,
    39→        scan_path=scan_path,
    40→        status_filter=status,
    41→        scope=opts.scope,
    42→        chronic=opts.chronic,
    43→    )
    44→    if opts.include_subjective and status == "all":
    45→        items += gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    46→    return items
    47→
    48→
    49→def items_for_visibility(*, snapshot, visibility: str) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    50→    """Select the snapshot partition for one queue surface."""
    51→    if visibility == QueueVisibility.BACKLOG:
    52→        return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.backlog_items]
    53→    return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.execution_items]
    54→
    55→
    56→def filter_snapshot_items(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    59→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    60→    """Apply view-local filtering after snapshot partition selection."""
    61→    filtered = items
    62→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    63→        filtered = [
    64→            item for item in filtered
    65→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
    66→        ]
    67→    if opts.scope:
    68→        filtered = [
    69→            item for item in filtered
    70→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension" or scope_matches(item, opts.scope)
    71→        ]
    72→    return filtered
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "filter_snapshot_items",
    77→    "items_for_visibility",
    78→    "select_queue_items",
    79→]
    80→
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE

    29→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    30→
    31→PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"
    32→PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"
    33→PHASE_SCAN = "scan"
    34→PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"
    35→PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"
    36→PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class QueueSnapshot:
    41→    """Canonical queue facts and partitions for one invocation."""
    42→
    43→    phase: str
    44→    all_objective_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    45→    all_initial_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    46→    all_postflight_review_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    47→    all_scan_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    48→    all_postflight_workflow_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    49→    all_postflight_triage_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    50→    execution_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    51→    backlog_items: tuple[WorkQueueItem, ...]
    52→    objective_in_scope_count: int
    53→    objective_execution_count: int
    54→    objective_backlog_count: int
    55→    subjective_initial_count: int
    56→    subjective_postflight_count: int
    57→    workflow_postflight_count: int
    58→    triage_pending_count: int
    59→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers: bool
    60→
    61→
    62→def _option_value(options: object | None, name: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    63→    if options is None:
    64→        return default
    65→    return getattr(options, name, default)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _resolved_scan_path(options: object | None, state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    69→    scan_path = _option_value(options, "scan_path", state.get("scan_path"))
    70→    if hasattr(scan_path, "__class__") and scan_path.__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState":
    71→        return state.get("scan_path")
    72→    return scan_path
    73→
    74→
    75→def _tracked_plan_ids(plan: dict | None) -> set[str]:
    76→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    77→        return set()
    78→    tracked: set[str] = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    79→    tracked.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    80→    tracked.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
    81→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    82→        tracked.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps", []):
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict):
    85→                tracked.update(step.get("issue_refs", []))
    86→    return tracked
    87→
    88→
    89→def _is_fresh_boundary(plan: dict | None) -> bool:
    90→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
    91→        return True
    92→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    93→    if not scores:
    94→        return True
    95→    return isinstance(scores, dict) and bool(scores.get("reset"))
    96→
    97→
    98→def _is_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem, *, skipped_ids: set[str]) -> bool:
    99→    return (
   100→        item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   101→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   102→        and item.get("id", "") not in skipped_ids
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _review_issue_items(items: Iterable[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   107→    return [
   108→        item for item in items
   109→        if item.get("detector", "") in {"review", "concerns", "subjective_review"}
   110→    ]
   111→
   112→
   113→def _subjective_partitions(
   114→    state: StateModel,
   115→    *,
   116→    scoped_issues: dict[str, dict],
   117→    threshold: float,
   118→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   119→    candidates = build_subjective_items(state, scoped_issues, threshold=threshold)
   120→    initial = [item for item in candidates if item.get("initial_review")]
   121→    postflight = [item for item in candidates if not item.get("initial_review")]
   122→    return initial, postflight
   123→
   124→
   125→def _workflow_partitions(
   126→    plan: dict | None,
   127→    state: StateModel,
   128→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   129→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   130→        return [], [], []
   131→    scan_items = [
   132→        item
   133→        for item in (
   134→            build_deferred_disposition_item(plan),
   135→            build_run_scan_item(plan),
   136→        )
   137→        if item is not None
   138→    ]
   139→    postflight_workflow = [
   140→        item
   141→        for item in (
   142→            build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state),
   143→            build_import_scores_item(plan, state),
   144→            build_communicate_score_item(plan, state),
   145→            build_create_plan_item(plan),
   146→        )
   147→        if item is not None
   148→    ]
   149→    triage_items = build_triage_stage_items(plan, state)
   150→    return scan_items, postflight_workflow, triage_items
   151→
   152→
   153→def _phase_for_snapshot(
   154→    *,
   155→    fresh_boundary: bool,
   156→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   157→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   158→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   159→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   160→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   161→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   162→) -> str:
   163→    if fresh_boundary and initial_review_items:
   164→        return PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL
   165→    if objective_items:
   166→        return PHASE_EXECUTE
   167→    if scan_items:
   168→        return PHASE_SCAN
   169→    if postflight_review_items:
   170→        return PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT
   171→    if postflight_workflow_items:
   172→        return PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT
   173→    if triage_items:
   174→        return PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT
   175→    return PHASE_SCAN
   176→
   177→
   178→def _execution_items_for_phase(
   179→    phase: str,
   180→    *,
   181→    objective_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   182→    initial_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   183→    scan_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   184→    postflight_review_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   185→    postflight_workflow_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   186→    triage_items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   187→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   188→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL:
   189→        return initial_review_items
   190→    if phase == PHASE_EXECUTE:
   191→        return objective_items
   192→    if phase == PHASE_SCAN:
   193→        deferred_items = [
   194→            item for item in scan_items
   195→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID
   196→        ]
   197→        if deferred_items:
   198→            return deferred_items
   199→        return [
   200→            item for item in scan_items
   201→            if item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID
   202→        ]
   203→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   204→        return postflight_review_items
   205→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   206→        return postflight_workflow_items
   207→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   208→        return triage_items
   209→    return []
   210→
   211→
   212→def build_queue_snapshot(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    *,
   215→    options: object | None = None,
   216→    plan: dict | None = None,
   217→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   218→) -> QueueSnapshot:
   219→    """Build the canonical queue snapshot for the current state."""
   220→    context = _option_value(options, "context", None)
   221→    effective_plan = context.plan if context is not None else (
   222→        plan if plan is not None else _option_value(options, "plan", None)
   223→    )
   224→    scan_path = _resolved_scan_path(options, state)
   225→    skipped_ids = set((effective_plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   226→    tracked_ids = _tracked_plan_ids(effective_plan)
   227→    scoped_issues = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   228→    scope = _option_value(options, "scope", None)
   229→    chronic = bool(_option_value(options, "chronic", False))
   230→
   231→    all_issue_items = build_issue_items(
   232→        state,
   233→        scan_path=scan_path,
   234→        status_filter="open",
   235→        scope=scope,
   236→        chronic=chronic,
   237→    )
   238→    objective_items = [
   239→        item for item in all_issue_items
   240→        if _is_objective_item(item, skipped_ids=skipped_ids)
   241→    ]
   242→    review_issue_items = _review_issue_items(all_issue_items)
   243→    initial_review_items, subjective_postflight_items = _subjective_partitions(
   244→        state,
   245→        scoped_issues=scoped_issues,
   246→        threshold=target_strict,
   247→    )
   248→    postflight_review_items = [*subjective_postflight_items, *review_issue_items]
   249→    scan_items, postflight_workflow_items, triage_items = _workflow_partitions(
   250→        effective_plan,
   251→        state,
   252→    )
   253→
   254→    fresh_boundary = _is_fresh_boundary(effective_plan)
   255→    phase = _phase_for_snapshot(
   256→        fresh_boundary=fresh_boundary,
   257→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   258→        objective_items=objective_items,
   259→        scan_items=scan_items,
   260→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   261→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   262→        triage_items=triage_items,
   263→    )
   264→    execution_items = _execution_items_for_phase(
   265→        phase,
   266→        objective_items=objective_items,
   267→        initial_review_items=initial_review_items,
   268→        scan_items=scan_items,
   269→        postflight_review_items=postflight_review_items,
   270→        postflight_workflow_items=postflight_workflow_items,
   271→        triage_items=triage_items,
   272→    )
   273→
   274→    execution_ids = {item.get("id", "") for item in execution_items}
   275→    backlog_items = [
   276→        item for item in (
   277→            [*objective_items, *initial_review_items, *postflight_review_items, *scan_items, *postflight_workflow_items, *triage_items]
   278→        )
   279→        if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   280→    ]
   281→    objective_backlog_count = sum(
   282→        1 for item in objective_items if item.get("id", "") not in execution_ids
   283→    )
   284→    has_unplanned_objective_blockers = any(
   285→        item.get("id", "") not in tracked_ids for item in objective_items
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    return QueueSnapshot(
   289→        phase=phase,
   290→        all_objective_items=tuple(objective_items),
   291→        all_initial_review_items=tuple(initial_review_items),
   292→        all_postflight_review_items=tuple(postflight_review_items),
   293→        all_scan_items=tuple(scan_items),
   294→        all_postflight_workflow_items=tuple(postflight_workflow_items),
   295→        all_postflight_triage_items=tuple(triage_items),
   296→        execution_items=tuple(execution_items),
   297→        backlog_items=tuple(backlog_items),
   298→        objective_in_scope_count=len(objective_items),
   299→        objective_execution_count=sum(
   300→            1
   301→            for item in execution_items
   302→            if item.get("kind") in {"issue", "cluster"}
   303→            and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   304→        ),
   305→        objective_backlog_count=objective_backlog_count,
   306→        subjective_initial_count=len(initial_review_items),
   307→        subjective_postflight_count=len(postflight_review_items),
   308→        workflow_postflight_count=len(postflight_workflow_items),
   309→        triage_pending_count=len(triage_items),
   310→        has_unplanned_objective_blockers=has_unplanned_objective_blockers,
   311→    )
   312→
   313→
   314→def coarse_phase_name(phase: str) -> str:
   315→    """Map internal queue phases to the persisted coarse lifecycle value."""
   316→    if phase == PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL or phase == PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT:
   317→        return "review"
   318→    if phase == PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT:
   319→        return "workflow"
   320→    if phase == PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT:
   321→        return "triage"
   322→    return phase
   323→
   324→
   325→__all__ = [
   326→    "PHASE_EXECUTE",
   327→    "PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL",
   328→    "PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT",
   329→    "PHASE_SCAN",
   330→    "PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT",
   331→    "PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT",
   332→    "QueueSnapshot",
   333→    "build_queue_snapshot",
   334→    "coarse_phase_name",
   335→]
   336→
```

```
+def _project_root_from_state_path(state_path_value: str | Path | None) -> Path | None:
+    """Infer project root from an explicit state path under ``.desloppify``."""
+    if state_path_value in (None, ""):
+        return None
+    try:
+        state_file = Path(state_path_value).resolve()
+    except OSError:
+        return None
+    if state_file.parent.name != ".desloppify":
+        return None
+    if state_file.name == "state.json" or (
+        state_file.name.startswith("state-") and state_file.suffix == ".json"
+    ):
+        return state_file.parent.parent
+    return None
+
+
 def _resolve_default_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
     """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.

+            if inferred is not None:
+                runtime.project_root = inferred
             _warn_if_running_installed_package_from_checkout()
             _resolve_default_path(args)
             _load_shared_runtime(args)
```

```
"""Lifecycle visibility filtering for work-queue items.

The queue has one explicit cycle:
``scan -> review -> workflow -> triage -> execute -> scan``.

Two details matter:
- ``review`` can be entered either from a fresh scan with initial subjective
  assessment placeholders, or after execute drains and post-flight review work
  exists. The phase name is the same, but the visible items differ.
- deferred disposition is treated as a blocker inside the ``scan`` boundary.
  It must be cleared before the scan step is considered available.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import (
    current_lifecycle_phase,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE,
    LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW,
    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID,
)
from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem

# Non-objective detectors that belong to the post-flight phase once objective
# execution work is drained.
# Must be a subset of NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS.
POSTFLIGHT_NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS


def _validate_postflight_non_objective_detectors() -> None:
    missing = POSTFLIGHT_NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS - NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "POSTFLIGHT_NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS has items not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: "
            f"{missing}"
        )


_validate_postflight_non_objective_detectors()


def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue.

    Checks both individual issues and collapsed clusters — clusters
    contain objective issues grouped by the queue builder.
    """
    return any(
        item.get("kind") in ("issue", "cluster")
        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
        for item in items
    )


def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
    return any(
        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
        and item.get("initial_review")
        for item in items
    )


def _is_postflight_non_objective_item(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    """True if this item belongs to the non-objective post-flight review phase."""
    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
        return not item.get("initial_review")
    return item.get("detector", "") in POSTFLIGHT_NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS


def _has_postflight_non_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    """True if any non-objective post-flight review items are pending."""
    return any(_is_postflight_non_objective_item(item) for item in items)


def _has_triage_stages(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    """True if any pending triage stage items are in the queue."""
    return any(
        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
        for item in items
    )


def _is_deferred_disposition(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    return item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID


def _has_deferred_disposition(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    return any(_is_deferred_disposition(item) for item in items)


def _is_postflight_scan(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    return item.get("id") == WORKFLOW_RUN_SCAN_ID


def _has_postflight_scan(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    return any(_is_postflight_scan(item) for item in items)


def _is_triage_stage(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    """True when item is a triage workflow stage."""
    return (
        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    )


def _is_postflight_workflow(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    return (
        item.get("kind") == "workflow_action"
        and not _is_deferred_disposition(item)
        and not _is_postflight_scan(item)
    )


def _has_postflight_workflow(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    return any(_is_postflight_workflow(item) for item in items)


def _is_force_visible(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    """True when the item is explicitly escalated past objective gating."""
    return bool(item.get("force_visible"))


def _is_postflight_phase_item(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    return (
        _is_postflight_non_objective_item(item)
        or _is_triage_stage(item)
        or _is_deferred_disposition(item)
        or _is_postflight_scan(item)
        or _is_postflight_workflow(item)
    )


def resolve_lifecycle_phase(
    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    *,
    plan: dict | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Resolve the active queue lifecycle phase from current items + plan state."""
    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
    if _has_objective_items(items):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE
    if _has_deferred_disposition(items) or _has_postflight_scan(items):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN
    if _has_postflight_non_objective_items(items):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW
    if _has_postflight_workflow(items):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW
    if _has_triage_stages(items):
        return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE
    persisted = current_lifecycle_phase(plan) if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
    if persisted is not None:
        return persisted
    return LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN


def apply_lifecycle_filter(
    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    *,
    plan: dict | None = None,
) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules from the resolved explicit phase."""
    phase = resolve_lifecycle_phase(items, plan=plan)

    if phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW:
        if _has_initial_reviews(items):
            return [
                item for item in items
                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review")
            ]
        return [
            item for item in items
            if _is_postflight_non_objective_item(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
        ]

    if phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE:
        return [
            item for item in items
            if not _is_postflight_phase_item(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
        ]

    if phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN:
        if _has_deferred_disposition(items):
            return [
                item for item in items
                if _is_deferred_disposition(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
            ]
        return [
            item for item in items
            if _is_postflight_scan(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
        ]

    if phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW:
        return [
            item for item in items
            if _is_postflight_workflow(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
        ]

    if phase == LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE:
        return [item for item in items if _is_triage_stage(item) or _is_force_visible(item)]

    # Explicit post-flight scan phase without an item should fall back to empty.
    if _has_postflight_scan(items):
        return [
            item for item in items
            if _is_postflight_scan(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
        ]
    return []


__all__ = ["apply_lifecycle_filter", "resolve_lifecycle_phase"]
```

```
1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.persistence import (
    18→    has_living_plan,
    19→    load_plan,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import PlanLoadStatus
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import QueueSnapshot, build_queue_snapshot
    24→
    25→
    26→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    27→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    28→
    29→
    30→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    31→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    32→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    33→
    34→
    35→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    36→class QueueContext:
    37→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    38→
    39→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through queue
    40→    builders and command helpers so every call site agrees on plan,
    41→    target, and canonical queue facts.
    42→    """
    43→
    44→    plan: dict | None
    45→    target_strict: float
    46→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    47→    snapshot: QueueSnapshot
    48→
    49→
    50→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    51→    *,
    52→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    53→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    54→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    55→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    56→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    57→
    58→    if not has_living_plan():
    59→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    60→
    61→    try:
    62→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    63→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    64→    except OSError as exc:
    65→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    66→            plan=None,
    67→            degraded=True,
    68→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    69→        )
    70→    except (
    71→        ValueError,
    72→        UnicodeDecodeError,
    73→    ) as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def queue_context(
    82→    state: StateModel,
    83→    *,
    84→    config: dict | None = None,
    85→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    86→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    87→) -> QueueContext:
    88→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    89→
    90→    Resolution order:
    91→
    92→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    93→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    94→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    95→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    96→    3. **snapshot** — canonical queue phase and partitions derived from the
    97→       resolved plan and target_strict.
    98→    """
    99→    # --- resolve plan ---
   100→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   101→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   102→
   103→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   104→    if target_strict is not None:
   105→        resolved_target = target_strict
   106→    elif config is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   108→    else:
   109→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   110→
   111→    snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
   112→        state,
   113→        plan=resolved_plan,
   114→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    return QueueContext(
   118→        plan=resolved_plan,
   119→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   120→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   121→        snapshot=snapshot,
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→__all__ = [
   126→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   127→    "QueueContext",
   128→    "queue_context",
   129→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   130→]
   131→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue entrypoints for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.finalize import finalize_queue
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import resolve_queue_inputs
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import (
    10→    QueueBuildOptions,
    11→    QueueVisibility,
    12→    ScanPathOption,
    13→    WorkQueueResult,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import group_queue_items
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.selection import select_queue_items
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    19→
    20→def build_work_queue(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    24→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    25→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    26→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    27→        state,
    28→        options=options,
    29→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,
    30→    )
    31→
    32→
    33→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    34→    state: StateModel,
    35→    *,
    36→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    37→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    38→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    39→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    40→
    41→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    42→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    43→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    44→    """
    45→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    46→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    47→    items = select_queue_items(
    48→        state,
    49→        opts=opts,
    50→        plan=plan,
    51→        scan_path=scan_path,
    52→        status=status,
    53→        threshold=threshold,
    54→        visibility=visibility,
    55→    )
    56→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)
    57→
    58→__all__ = [
    59→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
    60→    "QueueBuildOptions",
    61→    "QueueContext",
    62→    "QueueVisibility",
    63→    "WorkQueueResult",
    64→    "_build_work_queue_with_visibility",
    65→    "build_work_queue",
    66→    "collapse_clusters",
    67→    "group_queue_items",
    68→]
```

```
    57→        for node in ast.walk(tree):
    58→            if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
    59→                mod = node.module or ""
    60→                if mod.startswith("desloppify.engine._plan"):
    61→                    has_private_import = True
    62→                    break
    63→            elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
    64→                if any(
    65→                    alias.name.startswith("desloppify.engine._plan")
    66→                    for alias in node.names
    67→                ):
    68→                    has_private_import = True
    69→                    break
    70→        if has_private_import:
    71→            offenders.append(str(module_path.relative_to(package_root)))
    72→    return offenders
    73→
    74→
    75→def test_planning_and_work_queue_enforce_private_plan_boundary() -> None:
    76→    offenders = _private_plan_import_offenders("engine/planning")
    77→    offenders.extend(_private_plan_import_offenders("engine/_work_queue"))
    78→    assert offenders == []
    79→
```

> AGENT

So the `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_*` constants are `"scan"`, `"review"`, `"execute"`, `"workflow"`, `"triage"`. Now let me check what `snapshot.phase` returns vs. what `coarse_phase_name` maps to.

Looking at `snapshot.py`:
- `PHASE_REVIEW_INITIAL = "review_initial"` 
- `PHASE_EXECUTE = "execute"`
- `PHASE_SCAN = "scan"`
- `PHASE_REVIEW_POSTFLIGHT = "review_postflight"`
- `PHASE_WORKFLOW_POSTFLIGHT = "workflow_postflight"`
- `PHASE_TRIAGE_POSTFLIGHT = "triage_postflight"`

And `coarse_phase_name()` maps: `review_initial`/`review_postflight` -> `"review"`, `workflow_postflight` -> `"workflow"`, `triage_postflight` -> `"triage"`, otherwise returns the phase as-is (so `"execute"` and `"scan"` pass through).

But in `queue_progress.py`, `context.snapshot.phase` is used directly (NOT passed through `coarse_phase_name`). The comparisons there are against `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN` (`"scan"`), `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_EXECUTE` (`"execute"`), `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW` (`"review"`), etc.

This is a **bug**: `snapshot.phase` can be `"review_initial"` or `"review_postflight"` but the code compares to `"review"`. Same for `"workflow_postflight"` vs `"workflow"` and `"triage_postflight"` vs `"triage"`.

Here is my detailed review report:

---

## Code Review Report: 0.9.5 Branch Changes

### Test Results
**3 failed, 5121 passed, 3 skipped** out of 5124 tests.

---

### Issue 1 (BUG): Phase name mismatch in `queue_progress.py`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py`, lines 149-153

`context.snapshot.phase` returns fine-grained phase names from `snapshot.py` (e.g., `"review_initial"`, `"review_postflight"`, `"workflow_postflight"`, `"triage_postflight"`), but the code that consumes `lifecycle_phase` compares against the coarse `LIFECYCLE_PHASE_*` constants (`"review"`, `"workflow"`, `"triage"`).

The `objective_actionable` property (line 65) checks `self.lifecycle_phase in {LIFECYCLE_PHASE_SCAN, LIFECYCLE_PHASE_REVIEW, LIFECYCLE_PHASE_WORKFLOW, LIFECYCLE_PHASE_TRIAGE}`. With fine-grained phase values like `"review_initial"`, these comparisons will fail, causing incorrect score display mode decisions.

**Fix:** The snapshot phase should be passed through `coarse_phase_name()` (from `snapshot.py`) before assignment. The `plan_reconcile.py` file correctly does this (line 443), but `queue_progress.py` does not.

---

### Issue 2 (BUG): Duplicate test function name shadows earlier test

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/plan/test_plan_surface_contract_direct.py`, lines 41 and 52

Two functions are both named `test_plan_command_modules_avoid_legacy_plan_api_hub`. The second definition (line 52, which checks for `plan_queue` imports) shadows the first (line 41, which checks for `plan_api` imports). Python's class/module semantics mean the first test is silently never executed.

**Fix:** Rename one of the functions. The second one (checking for `plan_queue`) should likely be named something like `test_plan_command_modules_avoid_plan_queue_imports`.

---

### Issue 3 (FAILING TESTS): Architectural boundary contract tests

Three contract-enforcement tests fail because the refactoring intentionally changed import boundaries:

1. **`test_app_plan_modules_avoid_private_engine_plan_imports`** (`test_direct_coverage_priority_modules.py:155`) -- App modules like `resolve/living_plan.py`, `review/importing/plan_sync.py`, `scan/plan_reconcile.py`, etc. now import directly from `desloppify.engine._plan.*` instead of through the `plan_queue` facade. The test still enforces the old rule that app code must not import from `engine._plan`.

2. **`test_app_package_avoids_private_engine_plan_imports`** (`test_direct_coverage_priority_modules.py:163`) -- Same boundary: broad scan of all app modules for `engine._plan` imports.

3. **`test_planning_and_work_queue_enforce_private_plan_boundary`** (`test_planning_public_contract_direct.py:75`) -- The `_work_queue` package now imports from `engine._plan` (in `snapshot.py`, `synthetic.py`, `synthetic_workflow.py`, `context.py`), violating the old contract.

**Fix:** These tests need updating to reflect the new architecture where `engine.plan_queue` no longer exists as a facade. Either relax the boundary rules or create an explicit allowlist for the specific `_plan` submodules that `app` and `_work_queue` may import.

---

### Issue 4 (MINOR): Unused `policy` parameter in `_sync_lifecycle_phase_and_log`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py`, line 441

The `policy` keyword parameter is still accepted but no longer used (it was needed by the old `sync_lifecycle_phase` call). The function now uses `queue_context(state, plan=plan).snapshot.phase` instead. Dead parameter.

---

### Issue 5 (MINOR): String-based sentinel type checking

**Files:** `inputs.py` (line 22), `snapshot.py` (line 70)

Both use `__class__.__name__ == "_ScanPathFromState"` instead of `isinstance()`. This is done to avoid circular imports (`models.py` -> `context.py` -> `snapshot.py`). It works but is fragile -- if the class is renamed, these checks will silently break. Consider exporting a type-check helper from `models.py` or restructuring to avoid the cycle.

---

### Issue 6 (MINOR): `force_visible` removal may affect behavior

**Files:** `plan_order.py` (removed `force_visible_ids` stamping), `synthetic.py` (removed `force_visible` from triage items), `schema/__init__.py` (strips `force_visible_ids` from plan metadata)

The `force_visible` mechanism is being removed entirely. The old `lifecycle.py` used `_is_force_visible()` to exempt certain items from lifecycle filtering. The new `snapshot.py` has no equivalent -- there's no concept of force-visible items in the snapshot partitioning. If any plan state in the wild has `force_visible_ids` set, those escalations will be silently dropped. The schema migration (removing from `ensure_plan_defaults`) suggests this is intentional, but it's a behavioral change worth noting.

---

### What looks good

- All references to `plan_queue` and `lifecycle` modules have been properly migrated -- zero dangling imports remain for the deleted files.
- The new file decomposition (`models.py`, `inputs.py`, `selection.py`, `finalize.py`, `snapshot.py`) is clean with clear single-responsibility modules.
- The `QueueSnapshot` dataclass in `snapshot.py` is well-structured with comprehensive partition tracking.
- The `coarse_phase_name()` mapping function correctly bridges the gap between fine-grained snapshot phases and the persisted lifecycle phase values.
- Import chain within the new modules is acyclic.
- The `QueueBuildOptions` and `WorkQueueResult` types are correctly moved to `models.py` and re-exported from `core.py`.
- The CLI change adding `_project_root_from_state_path` is clean and correct.
- The website change (`DOMContentLoaded` -> `load`) is fine.

```
    62→def _serialize_issue_item_base(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    63→    """Serialize core issue fields shared across output modes."""
    64→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    65→        "id": item.get("id"),
    66→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    67→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    68→        "file": item.get("file"),
    69→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    70→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    71→        "status": item.get("status"),
    72→    }
    73→    serialized["kind"] = item.get("kind", "issue")
    74→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    75→    return serialized
    76→
    77→
    78→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    79→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    80→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    81→        return _serialize_cluster_item(item)
    82→
    83→    serialized = _serialize_issue_item_base(item)
    84→
    85→    # Workflow state
    86→    blocked_by = item.get("blocked_by")
    87→    if blocked_by:
    88→        serialized["blocked_by"] = blocked_by
    89→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    90→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    91→    explain = item.get("explain")
    92→    if explain is not None:
    93→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    94→
    95→    # Plan metadata
    96→    for key in ("queue_position", "plan_description", "plan_note", "plan_cluster"):
    97→        value = item.get(key)
    98→        if value:
    99→            serialized[key] = value
   100→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   101→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
   102→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
   103→        skip_reason = item.get("plan_skip_reason")
   104→        if skip_reason:
   105→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   106→
   107→    return serialized
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_query_payload(
   111→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
   112→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   113→    *,
   114→    command: str,
   115→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   116→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   117→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   118→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   119→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   120→    mode = "execution" if command == "next" else command
   121→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   122→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   123→        "mode": mode,
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    agent_notes = [
   127→        "Do NOT use `desloppify plan skip` unless the user explicitly asks you to skip an item.",
   128→        "If you cannot fix an item, report it and move to the next one.",
   129→        "For cluster items: if the autofix_hint finds 0 results, drill into individual issues with the primary_command.",
   130→    ]
   131→    if command == "backlog":
   132→        agent_notes = [
   133→            "This is backlog discovery, not the active execution queue.",
   134→            "Use `desloppify next` for the current execution item.",
   135→            "Do NOT treat backlog rank as permission to bypass the living plan.",
   136→        ]
   137→
   138→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   139→        "command": command,
   140→        "items": serialized,
   141→        "queue": queue_section,
   142→        "narrative": narrative,
   143→        "agent_notes": agent_notes,
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    if plan and (
   147→        plan.get("queue_order")
   148→        or plan.get("skipped")
   149→        or plan.get("clusters")
   150→    ):
   151→        clusters_summary = []
   152→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   153→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   154→            clusters_summary.append({
   155→                "name": name,
   156→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   157→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   158→            })
   159→        payload["plan"] = {
   160→            "active": True,
   161→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   162→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   163→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   164→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   165→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   166→        }
   167→
   168→    return payload
   169→
   170→
   171→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   172→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   173→    return render_markdown_for_command(items, command="next")
   174→
   175→
   176→def render_markdown_for_command(
   177→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   178→    *,
   179→    command: str,
   180→) -> str:
   181→    """Render queue items as markdown for the given queue surface."""
   182→    heading = "# Desloppify Execution Queue"
   183→    if command == "backlog":
   184→        heading = "# Desloppify Backlog"
   185→    lines = [
   186→        heading,
   187→        "",
   188→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   189→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   190→    ]
   191→    for item in items:
   192→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   193→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   194→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   195→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   196→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   197→    lines.append("")
   198→    return "\n".join(lines)
   199→
   200→
   201→def write_output_file(
   202→    output_file: str,
   203→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   204→    item_count: int,
   205→    *,
   206→    safe_write_text_fn,
   207→    colorize_fn,
   208→    label: str = "queue output",
   209→) -> bool:
   210→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   211→    try:
   212→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   213→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   214→    except OSError as exc:
   215→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   216→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label=label)
   217→        return False
   218→    return True
   219→
   220→
   221→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   222→    output_format: str,
   223→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   224→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   225→    *,
   226→    command: str = "next",
   227→) -> bool:
   228→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   229→    renderers = {
   230→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   231→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown_for_command(items, command=command)),
   232→    }
   233→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   234→    if renderer is None:
   235→        return False
   236→    renderer()
   237→    return True
   238→
   239→
   240→__all__ = [
   241→    "build_query_payload",
   242→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   243→    "render_markdown",
   244→    "render_markdown_for_command",
   245→    "serialize_item",
   246→    "write_output_file",
   247→]
   248→
```

```
   281→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})

   283→    total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   284→    desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   285→    print(colorize(f"\n  ┌─ Cluster: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining) ─┐", "cyan"))
   286→    if desc:
   287→        print(colorize(f"  │ {desc}", "cyan"))
   288→    steps = cluster_data.get("action_steps") or []
   289→    if steps:
   290→        print(colorize("  │", "cyan"))
   291→        print(colorize("  │ Action plan:", "cyan"))
   292→        for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   293→            print(colorize(f"  │   {idx}. {_step_text(step)}", "cyan"))
   294→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 60 + "┘", "cyan"))
   295→    print(colorize("  Back to full queue: desloppify next", "dim"))
   296→    if steps:
   297→        print(colorize(f"  Mark step done: desloppify plan cluster update {cluster_name} --done-step N", "dim"))
   298→    return bool(steps)

   301→def render_terminal_items(
   302→    items: list[dict],
   303→    dim_scores: dict,
   304→    issues_scoped: dict,
   305→    *,
   306→    group: str,
   307→    explain: bool,
   308→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   309→    plan: dict | None = None,
   310→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   311→) -> None:
   312→    header_showed_plan = False
   313→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   314→    if effective_cluster and plan:
   315→        header_showed_plan = _render_cluster_drill_header(
   316→            items=items,
   317→            plan=plan,
   318→            cluster_name=effective_cluster,
   319→        )
   320→
   321→    if group != "item":
   322→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   323→        return
   324→
   325→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and bool(effective_cluster)
   326→
   327→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   328→        if idx > 0:
   329→            print()
   330→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   331→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   332→            continue
   333→        label = _item_label(item, idx, len(items))
   334→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   335→        _render_item(
   336→            item, dim_scores, issues_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials,
   337→            single_item=len(items) == 1,
   338→            header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   339→        )
   340→
   341→
   342→__all__ = [
   343→    "render_terminal_items",
   344→]
```

```
    14→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    16→from desloppify.engine.planning.queue_policy import build_execution_queue
    17→
    18→from .options import NextOptions
    19→from .queue_flow import build_and_render_execution_queue
    20→from .subjective import _low_subjective_dimensions
    21→
    22→# Backward-compatible test seam: `next` now uses the execution queue wrapper.
    23→build_work_queue = build_execution_queue
    24→
    25→
    26→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    27→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    28→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    29→    state = runtime.state
    30→    config = runtime.config
    31→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    35→    if skill_warning:
    36→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    37→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    38→    if config_warning:
    39→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    40→
    41→    if getattr(args, "format", "terminal") == "terminal":
    42→        print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    43→    build_and_render_execution_queue(
    44→        args,
    45→        state,
    46→        config,
    47→        resolve_lang_fn=resolve_lang,
    48→        load_plan_fn=load_plan,
    49→        build_work_queue_fn=build_work_queue,
    50→        write_query_fn=write_query,
    51→    )
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = [
    55→    "NextOptions",
    56→    "_low_subjective_dimensions",
    57→    "build_work_queue",
    58→    "build_execution_queue",
    59→    "build_and_render_execution_queue",
    60→    "cmd_next",
    61→    "load_plan",
    62→    "resolve_lang",
    63→    "write_query",
    64→]
```

```
    61→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    62→    cmd = (command or "").strip()

    64→
    65→
    66→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    67→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    68→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    69→        return "[review]"
    70→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):

    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    79→        for item in grouped_items:

    81→            tag = effort_tag(item)

    84→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    85→            )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:
    89→    if cluster_name in CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names:
    90→        return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names[cluster_name]
    91→    return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.action_types.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
    92→
    93→
    94→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:
    95→    file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
    96→    if len(file_counts) <= 5:
    97→        print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
    98→        for filename, count in file_counts.most_common():
    99→            print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   103→    for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
   104→        print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   105→    remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   106→    print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _render_cluster_sample(members: list[dict]) -> None:
   110→    print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   111→    for member in members[:3]:
   112→        summary = member.get("summary") or member.get("id", "")
   113→        print(f"    - {summary}")
   114→    if len(members) > 3:
   115→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   116→
   117→
   118→def cluster_action_commands(cluster_name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   119→    """Return semantic cluster action commands independent of terminal labels."""
   120→    return {
   121→        "resolve_all": f'desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   122→        "drill_in": f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   123→        "skip": f"desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}",
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   128→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   129→    print(colorize(f"\n  Skip:          {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   130→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   131→    print(
   132→        colorize(
   133→            f"  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   140→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   141→    print(
   142→        colorize(
   143→            f"\n  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   144→            "dim",
   145→        )
   146→    )
   147→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   149→
   150→
   151→def _step_display_text(step: str | dict) -> str:
   152→    """Extract display text from an action step (string or dict with title)."""
   153→    if isinstance(step, dict):
   154→        return step.get("title", str(step))
   155→    return str(step)
   156→
   157→
   158→def _cluster_header_bits(item: dict) -> tuple[str, str, str, list[dict]]:
   159→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   160→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   161→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   162→    done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   163→    step_badge = f" [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps done]" if action_steps else ""
   164→    optional_tag = " — optional" if item.get("cluster_optional") else ""
   165→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   166→    return cluster_name, type_label, f"{step_badge}{optional_tag}", action_steps
   167→
   168→
   169→def _render_cluster_steps(action_steps: list[dict]) -> None:
   170→    if not action_steps:
   171→        return
   172→    show_count = min(3, len(action_steps))
   173→    for i, step in enumerate(action_steps[:show_count], 1):
   174→        marker = "[x]" if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("done") else "[ ]"
   175→        print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   176→    remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
   177→    if remaining > 0:
   178→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more — drill in to view all", "dim"))
   179→
   180→
   181→def _render_cluster_priority(dep_order: object) -> None:
   182→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:
   183→        print(colorize("  Priority: complete before other clusters", "cyan"))
   184→
   185→
   186→def _render_cluster_primary_action(item: dict) -> None:
   187→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
   188→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   189→    if autofix_hint:
   190→        print(colorize(f"\n  Try auto first: {autofix_hint}", "cyan"))
   191→        print(colorize("  If auto finds 0, drill into individual issues:", "dim"))
   192→    if primary_command:
   193→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   194→
   195→
   196→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   197→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   198→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   199→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   200→    cluster_name, type_label, suffix, action_steps = _cluster_header_bits(item)
   201→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{suffix})", "bold"))
   202→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   203→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   204→
   205→    _render_cluster_steps(action_steps)
   206→    _render_cluster_priority(item.get("dependency_order"))
   207→
   208→    members = item.get("members", [])
   209→    if members:
   210→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   211→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   212→
   213→    _render_cluster_primary_action(item)
   214→
   215→    if is_optional:
   216→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   217→        return
   218→
   219→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   220→
   221→
   222→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   223→    del explain
   224→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   225→    print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   226→    if total > 5:
   227→        print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))
   228→
   229→
   230→def show_empty_queue(
   231→    queue: dict,
   232→    strict: float | None,
   233→    *,
   234→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   235→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   236→) -> bool:
   237→    del target_strict
   238→    if queue.get("items"):
   239→        return False
   240→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   241→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   242→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   243→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   244→        print(colorize(
   245→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   246→            "cyan",
   247→        ))
   248→        print(colorize(
   249→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   250→            "dim",
   251→        ))
   252→        return True
   253→
   254→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   255→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   256→    return True
   257→
   258→
   259→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   260→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   261→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   262→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   263→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   264→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   265→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   266→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   267→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   268→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   269→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   270→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   271→
```

```
1→"""Workflow item rendering helpers for next terminal output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
     6→    triage_manual_stage_command,
     7→    triage_run_stages_command,
     8→)
     9→
    10→
    11→def step_text(step: str | dict) -> str:
    12→    if isinstance(step, dict):
    13→        return step.get("title", str(step))
    14→    return str(step)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _detail_mapping(item: dict) -> dict:
    18→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
    19→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    20→
    21→
    22→def _print_command_list(
    23→    title: str,
    24→    entries: object,
    25→    *,
    26→    colorize_fn,
    27→) -> None:
    28→    if not isinstance(entries, list) or not entries:
    29→        return
    30→    print(colorize_fn(title, "dim"))
    31→    for idx, entry in enumerate(entries, 1):
    32→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    33→            continue
    34→        label = str(entry.get("label", "")).strip()
    35→        command = str(entry.get("command", "")).strip()
    36→        if label and command:
    37→            print(colorize_fn(f"  {idx}. {label}: {command}", "dim"))
    38→        elif command:
    39→            print(colorize_fn(f"  {idx}. {command}", "dim"))
    40→        elif label:
    41→            print(colorize_fn(f"  {idx}. {label}", "dim"))
    42→
    43→
    44→def _print_blocked_stage_actions(
    45→    *,
    46→    blocked_by: list[str],
    47→    colorize_fn,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    deps = ", ".join(dep.replace("triage::", "") for dep in blocked_by)
    50→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Blocked by: {deps}", "dim"))
    51→    first_dep = blocked_by[0] if blocked_by else ""
    52→    dep_name = first_dep.replace("triage::", "")
    53→    if not dep_name:
    54→        return
    55→    print(
    56→        colorize_fn(
    57→            f"  Next step: {triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=dep_name)}",
    58→            "dim",
    59→        )
    60→    )
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize_fn(
    63→            f"  Alt runner: {triage_run_stages_command(runner='claude', only_stages=dep_name)}",
    64→            "dim",
    65→        )
    66→    )
    67→    print(
    68→        colorize_fn(
    69→            f"  Manual fallback: {triage_manual_stage_command(dep_name)}",
    70→            "dim",
    71→        )
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→def _print_runner_commands(detail: dict, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
    76→    _print_command_list("  Runners:", detail.get("runner_commands", []), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
    77→    manual_fallback = detail.get("manual_fallback", "")
    78→    if manual_fallback:
    79→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Manual fallback: {manual_fallback}", "dim"))
    80→
    81→
    82→def render_workflow_stage(item: dict, *, colorize_fn, workflow_stage_name_fn) -> None:
    83→    """Render a triage workflow stage item."""
    84→    blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    85→    detail = _detail_mapping(item)
    86→    stage = workflow_stage_name_fn(item)
    87→    tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    88→    style = "dim" if blocked else "bold"
    89→    print(colorize_fn(f"  (Planning stage: {stage}{tag})", style))
    90→    print(colorize_fn("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    91→    print(f"  {colorize_fn(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    92→    total = detail.get("total_review_issues", 0)
    93→    if total:
    94→        print(colorize_fn(f"  {total} review issues to analyze", "dim"))
    95→    if blocked:
    96→        _print_blocked_stage_actions(
    97→            blocked_by=item.get("blocked_by", []),
    98→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
    99→        )
   100→    else:
   101→        print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', '')}", "cyan"))
   102→        _print_runner_commands(detail, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   103→
   104→
   105→def render_workflow_action(item: dict, *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   106→    print(colorize_fn("  (Workflow step)", "bold"))
   107→    print(colorize_fn("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   108→    print(f"  {colorize_fn(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   109→    detail = _detail_mapping(item)
   110→    _print_command_list(
   111→        "\n  Planning tools:",
   112→        detail.get("planning_tools", []),
   113→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   114→    )
   115→    _print_command_list(
   116→        "\n  Decision options:",
   117→        detail.get("decision_options", []),
   118→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   119→    )
   120→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', '')}", "cyan"))
```

```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class ReviewOptions:
    25→    """All user-facing review command options extracted once from argparse."""
    26→
    27→    merge: bool = False
    28→    run_batches: bool = False
    29→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    30→    external_start: bool = False
    31→    external_submit: bool = False
    32→    import_file: str | None = None
    33→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    34→    session_id: str | None = None
    35→    allow_partial: bool = False
    36→    scan_after_import: bool = False
    37→    path: str = "."
    38→    dry_run: bool = False
    39→    manual_override: bool = False
    40→    attested_external: bool = False
    41→    attest: str | None = None
    42→
    43→    @classmethod
    44→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    45→        return cls(
    46→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    47→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    48→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    49→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    50→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    51→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    52→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    53→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    54→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    55→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    56→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    57→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
    58→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
    59→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
    60→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
    61→        )
    62→
    63→
    64→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    65→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    66→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    67→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    68→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    69→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    70→            continue
    71→        try:
    72→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    73→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    74→            _ = exc
    75→
    76→
    77→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    78→    if lang:
    79→        return
    80→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    84→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    85→    return [
    86→        opts.merge,
    87→        opts.run_batches,
    88→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    89→        opts.external_start,
    90→        opts.external_submit,
    91→        import_mode,
    92→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    93→    ]
    94→
    95→
    96→def _validate_mode_selection(
    97→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    98→    *,
    99→    mode_flags: list[bool],
   100→) -> None:
   101→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   102→        raise CommandError(
   103→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   104→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   105→            exit_code=1,
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   109→        raise CommandError(
   110→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   111→            exit_code=2,
   112→        )
   113→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   114→        raise CommandError(
   115→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   116→            exit_code=2,
   117→        )
   118→
   119→
   120→def _is_default_prepare_mode(opts: ReviewOptions, mode_flags: list[bool]) -> bool:
   121→    """True when the command will run the default prepare flow (no explicit mode)."""
   122→    return not any(mode_flags) and not opts.import_file
   123→
   124→
   125→def _run_review_mode(
   126→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   127→    *,
   128→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   129→    runtime,
   130→    state,
   131→    lang,
   132→    state_file,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if opts.merge:
   135→        do_merge(args)
   136→        return
   137→    if opts.run_batches:
   138→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   139→        do_run_batches(
   140→            args,
   141→            state,
   142→            lang,
   143→            state_file,
   144→            config=runtime.config,
   145→        )
   146→        return
   147→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   148→        do_import_run(
   149→            opts.import_run_dir,
   150→            state,
   151→            lang,
   152→            state_file,
   153→            config=runtime.config,
   154→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   155→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   156→            scan_path=opts.path,
   157→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   158→        )
   159→        return
   160→    if opts.external_start:
   161→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   162→        do_external_start(
   163→            args,
   164→            state,
   165→            lang,
   166→            config=runtime.config,
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→    if opts.external_submit:
   170→        do_external_submit(
   171→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   172→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   173→            state=state,
   174→            lang=lang,
   175→            state_file=state_file,
   176→            config=runtime.config,
   177→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   178→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   179→            scan_path=opts.path,
   180→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   181→        )
   182→        return
   183→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   184→        do_validate_import(
   185→            opts.validate_import_file,
   186→            lang,
   187→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   188→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   189→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   190→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   191→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   192→            ),
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    if opts.import_file:
   197→        do_import(
   198→            opts.import_file,
   199→            state,
   200→            lang,
   201→            state_file,
   202→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   203→                config=runtime.config,
   204→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   205→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   206→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   207→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   208→            ),
   209→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   210→        )
   211→        return
   212→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   213→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   214→
   215→
   216→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   217→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   218→    _enable_live_review_output()
   219→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   220→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   221→    state = runtime.state
   222→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   223→    _require_lang(lang)
   224→
   225→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   226→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   227→    _validate_mode_selection(
   228→        opts,
   229→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # Default prepare and --run-batches need real scan-backed context rather
   233→    # than reconstructed plan inventory.
   234→    if _is_default_prepare_mode(opts, mode_flags) or opts.run_batches:
   235→        if not require_scan_metrics(state):
   236→            return
   237→
   238→    _run_review_mode(
   239→        args,
   240→        opts=opts,
   241→        runtime=runtime,
   242→        state=state,
   243→        lang=lang,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→    )
   246→
```

```
    65→def _utc_now() -> datetime:

    67→

    69→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:

    71→
    72→
    73→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    74→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    75→        return None
    76→    try:
    77→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    78→    except ValueError:
    79→        return None
    80→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    81→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    82→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)
```
