```
    97→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    98→    if not session_id.strip():
    99→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
   100→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
   101→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
   102→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)

   104→
   105→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   106→    if not path.exists():
   107→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
   108→    try:
   109→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   110→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   111→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
   112→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   113→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
   114→    return payload
   115→
   116→
   117→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   118→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   119→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   120→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   121→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   122→    if payload_id != session_id:
   123→        raise CommandError(
   124→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   125→        )
   126→    return path, payload

   129→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(

   141→            state=state,

   152→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1) from exc

   264→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'

   266→        '    "concern_verdict": "confirmed|dismissed  // for concern signals only",\n'
   267→        '    "concern_fingerprint": "abc123  // required when dismissed; copy from signal fingerprint",\n'
   268→        '    "reasoning": "why dismissed  // optional, for dismissed only"\n'
   269→        "  }]\n"
   270→        "}\n\n"
   271→    )
   272→
   273→    session_requirements = (
   274→        "Session requirements:\n"
   275→        f"1. Keep `session.id` exactly `{session_id}`.\n"
   276→        f"2. Keep `session.token` exactly `{token}`.\n"
   277→        "3. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).\n"
   278→    )
   279→
   280→    from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_policy_result, render_policy_block
   281→
   282→    policy_result = load_policy_result()
   283→    policy_text = render_policy_block(policy_result.policy)
   284→    if not policy_result.ok:
   285→        print(
   286→            colorize(
   287→                f"  Warning: ignoring malformed project policy ({policy_result.message or 'unknown error'}).",
   288→                "yellow",
   289→            )
   290→        )
   291→
   292→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   293→        header,
   294→        *batch_sections,
   295→        policy_text,
   296→        render_scoring_frame(),
   297→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   298→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=combined_cap, dim_set=all_dims),
   299→        render_scope_enums(),
   300→        output_schema,
   301→        session_requirements,
   302→    )

   305→def do_external_start(args, state, lang, *, config: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   306→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""
   307→    config = config or {}
   308→    runner = str(getattr(args, "external_runner", "claude")).strip().lower()
   309→    if runner not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   310→        raise CommandError(
   311→            f"Error: unsupported external runner '{runner}'. Supported: claude.",
   312→            exit_code=2,
   313→        )
   314→    ttl_hours = int(getattr(args, "session_ttl_hours", 24) or 0)
   315→    if ttl_hours <= 0:
   316→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-ttl-hours must be > 0.", exit_code=2)
   317→
   318→    packet, packet_path, blind_path = _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   319→        args,
   320→        state,

   322→        config=config,
   323→    )
   324→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_path)
   325→    if not isinstance(packet_hash, str):
   326→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed to hash blind packet: {blind_path}")
   327→
   328→    now = _utc_now()
   329→    expires = now + timedelta(hours=ttl_hours)
   330→    session_id = _session_id()
   331→    [REDACTED](16)

   334→

   336→    template_path = session_dir / "review_result.template.json"

   338→    launch_prompt_path = session_dir / "claude_launch_prompt.md"

   359→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   360→    safe_write_text(template_path, json.dumps(template_payload, indent=2) + "\n")

   426→
   427→def _canonical_external_payload(
   428→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   429→    *,
   430→    session: dict[str, Any],
   431→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   432→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   433→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   434→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   435→        raise CommandError(
   436→            "Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object."
   437→            ' Expected: {"session":{"id":"...","token":"..."},"assessments":{...},"issues":[...]}',
   438→        )
   439→
   440→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   441→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   442→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   443→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   444→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   445→        raise CommandError(
   446→            "Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload."
   447→            " Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.",
   448→        )
   449→
   450→    payload = {
   451→        key: value
   452→        for key, value in raw_payload.items()
   453→        if key not in {"session", "provenance"}
   454→    }
   455→    payload["provenance"] = {
   456→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   457→        "blind": True,
   458→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   459→        "session_id": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   460→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   461→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   462→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   463→    }
   464→    return payload
   465→
   466→
   467→def _ensure_session_open(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   468→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   469→    if status == "open":
   470→        return
   471→    raise CommandError(
   472→        f"Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   473→    )
   474→
   475→
   476→def _ensure_session_not_expired(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   477→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   478→    if expires_at is None:
   479→        raise CommandError(
   480→            "Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   481→        )
   482→    now = _utc_now()
   483→    if now <= expires_at:
   484→        return
   485→    raise CommandError(
   486→        f"Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   487→    )
   488→
   489→
   490→def do_external_submit(
   491→    *,
   492→    import_file: str,
   493→    session_id: str,
   494→    state: dict,
   495→    lang,
   496→    state_file,
   497→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   498→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   499→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   500→    scan_path: str = ".",
   501→    dry_run: bool = False,
   502→) -> None:
   503→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   504→    config = config or {}
   505→    session_path, session = _session_payload(session_id)
   506→    _ensure_session_open(session)
   507→    _ensure_session_not_expired(session)
   508→
   509→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   510→        raise CommandError(
   511→            "Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   512→        )
   513→
   514→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   515→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(issues_path, label="external issues")
   516→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   517→
   518→    stamp = run_stamp()
   519→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   520→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   521→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   522→
   523→    _import_config = ReviewImportConfig(
   524→        config=config,
   525→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   526→        attested_external=True,
   527→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   528→    )
   529→
   530→    if dry_run:
   531→        do_validate_import(
   532→            str(canonical_path),
   533→            lang,
   534→            import_config=_import_config,
   535→        )
   536→        return
   537→
   538→    do_import(
   539→        str(canonical_path),
   540→        state,
   541→        lang,
   542→        state_file,
   543→        import_config=_import_config,
   544→    )
   545→
   546→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   547→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   548→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   549→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(issues_path)
   550→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   551→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   552→
   553→    if scan_after_import:
   554→        code = run_followup_scan(
   555→            lang_name=lang.name,
   556→            scan_path=scan_path,
   557→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   558→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   559→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   560→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   561→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   562→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   563→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   564→            ),
   565→        )
   566→        if code != 0:
   567→            raise CommandError(f"External review exited with code {code}", exit_code=code)
   568→
   569→
   570→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   571→
```

```
    32→class _DetectorNamesCacheCompat:
    33→    """Compat shim for tests that poke the legacy detector-name cache."""
    34→
    35→    def __init__(self) -> None:
    36→        self._store: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    37→
    38→    def __contains__(self, key: object) -> bool:
    39→        return key in self._store
    40→
    41→    def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> list[str]:
    42→        return self._store[key]
    43→
    44→    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: list[str]) -> None:
    45→        self._store[key] = value
    46→
    47→    def pop(self, key: str, default: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str] | None:
    48→        return self._store.pop(key, default)

    79→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(
    80→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    81→    config: Mapping[str, Any],
    82→) -> None:
    83→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    84→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    85→    persisted_raw = config.get("exclude", [])
    86→    persisted = (
    87→        [entry for entry in persisted_raw if isinstance(entry, str)]
    88→        if isinstance(persisted_raw, list)
    89→        else []
    90→    )
    91→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    92→    if not combined:
    93→        return
    94→    set_exclusions(combined)
    95→    if cli_exclusions:
    96→        print(
    97→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    98→            file=sys.stderr,
    99→        )
   100→        return
   101→    print(
   102→        colorize(
   103→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
   104→        ),
   105→        file=sys.stderr,
   106→    )

   109→def _project_root_from_state_path(state_path_value: str | Path | None) -> Path | None:
   110→    """Infer project root from an explicit state path under ``.desloppify``."""
   111→    if state_path_value in (None, ""):
   112→        return None
   113→    try:
   114→        state_file = Path(state_path_value).resolve()
   115→    except OSError:
   116→        return None
   117→    if state_file.parent.name != ".desloppify":
   118→        return None
   119→    if state_file.name == "state.json" or (
   120→        state_file.name.startswith("state-") and state_file.suffix == ".json"
   121→    ):
   122→        return state_file.parent.parent
   123→    return None
   124→
   125→
   126→def _resolve_default_path(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   127→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
   128→
   129→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
   130→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
   131→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
   132→    """
   133→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
   134→        return
   135→    runtime_root = get_project_root()
   136→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
   137→        try:
   138→            state_file = state_path(args)
   139→            if state_file:
   140→                saved = load_state(state_file)
   141→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
   142→                if saved_path:
   143→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
   144→                    return
   145→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
   146→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "resolve default review path from saved state", exc)
   147→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   148→    if lang:
   149→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   150→    else:
   151→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
   152→
   153→
   154→def _load_shared_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   155→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   156→    config = load_config()
   157→
   158→    state_file = state_path(args)
   159→    state = load_state(state_file)
   160→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   161→
   162→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   163→
   164→
   165→def _looks_like_desloppify_checkout(root: Path) -> bool:
   166→    """Return True when *root* appears to be the desloppify source checkout."""
   167→    package_dir = root / "desloppify"
   168→    pyproject = root / "pyproject.toml"
   169→    if not package_dir.is_dir() or not (package_dir / "__init__.py").is_file():
   170→        return False
   171→    if not pyproject.is_file():
   172→        return False
   173→    try:
   174→        text = pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   175→    except OSError:
   176→        return False
   177→    return 'name = "desloppify"' in text or "name='desloppify'" in text
   178→
   179→
   180→def _running_installed_package_from_checkout(
   181→    *,
   182→    cwd_root: Path | None = None,
   183→    module_file: str | Path | None = None,
   184→) -> bool:
   185→    """Detect running an installed package while standing in a repo checkout."""
   186→    root = (cwd_root or get_project_root()).resolve()
   187→    if not _looks_like_desloppify_checkout(root):
   188→        return False
   189→    current_module = Path(module_file or __file__).resolve()
   190→    try:
   191→        current_module.relative_to(root)
   192→        return False
   193→    except ValueError:
   194→        return True
   195→
   196→
   197→def _warn_if_running_installed_package_from_checkout() -> None:
   198→    """Emit a warning when CLI resolution bypasses the current checkout."""
   199→    if not _running_installed_package_from_checkout():
   200→        return
   201→    root = get_project_root().resolve()
   202→    print(
   203→        colorize(
   204→            "  WARNING: running installed desloppify package while current directory "
   205→            "looks like the desloppify checkout.",
   206→            "yellow",
   207→        ),
   208→        file=sys.stderr,
   209→    )
   210→    print(
   211→        colorize(
   212→            f"  Current checkout: {root}",
   213→            "dim",
   214→        ),
   215→        file=sys.stderr,
   216→    )
   217→    print(
   218→        colorize(
   219→            "  Use `python -m desloppify ...` or `./.venv/bin/desloppify ...` here "
   220→            "to run the local checkout instead of the installed package.",
   221→            "dim",
   222→        ),
   223→        file=sys.stderr,
   224→    )
   225→
   226→
   227→def _resolve_handler(command: str) -> CommandHandler:
   228→    """Resolve a CLI command name to its typed handler callable."""
   229→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   230→
   231→
   232→def _handle_help_command(
   233→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   234→    parser: argparse.ArgumentParser,
   235→) -> None:
   236→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   237→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   238→    try:
   239→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   240→    except SystemExit:
   241→        return
   242→
   243→
   244→def main() -> None:
   245→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   246→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   247→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   248→            try:
   249→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   250→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   251→                logger.debug(
   252→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   253→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   254→                )
   255→
   256→    parser = create_parser()
   257→    args = parser.parse_args()
   258→    if not args.command:
   259→        parser.print_help()
   260→        return
   261→    if args.command == "help":
   262→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   263→        return
   264→
   265→    try:
   266→        with runtime_scope() as runtime:
   267→            inferred = _project_root_from_state_path(getattr(args, "state", None))
   268→            if inferred is not None:
   269→                runtime.project_root = inferred
   270→            _warn_if_running_installed_package_from_checkout()
   271→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   272→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   273→
   274→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   275→            handler(args)
   276→    except CommandError as exc:
   277→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   278→        sys.exit(exc.exit_code)
   279→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   280→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   281→        sys.exit(1)
   282→
   283→
   284→if __name__ == "__main__":
   285→    main()
   286→
```

```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective issues and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import load_query_result
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    17→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel, save_state
    18→
    19→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    20→
    21→
    22→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    23→    state: StateModel,
    24→    *,
    25→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    26→) -> list[str]:
    27→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    28→
    29→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    30→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    31→
    32→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    33→    """
    34→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    35→    cleared: list[str] = []
    36→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    40→            continue
    41→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    42→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    44→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    45→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    46→    return cleared
    47→
    48→
    49→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    51→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    52→    scored: list[str] = []
    53→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    54→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    55→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    56→                scored.append(dim_key)
    57→        elif isinstance(assessment, int | float) and assessment:
    58→            scored.append(dim_key)
    59→    return sorted(scored)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    63→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    64→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    65→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _prepared_query_dimensions() -> set[str] | None:
    69→    """Return prepared review dimensions from query.json when available."""
    70→    query_result = load_query_result()
    71→    if not query_result.ok:
    72→        if query_result.error_kind in {"query_parse_error", "query_invalid_shape"}:
    73→            detail = query_result.message or query_result.error_kind
    74→            print(
    75→                colorize(
    76→                    f"  Warning: ignoring invalid query artifact ({detail}).",
    77→                    "yellow",
    78→                ),
    79→                file=sys.stderr,
    80→            )
    81→        return None
    82→    query = query_result.payload or {}
    83→    if not isinstance(query, dict):
    84→        return None
    85→    if query.get("command") != "review" or query.get("mode") != "holistic":
    86→        return None
    87→    if not isinstance(query.get("investigation_batches"), list):
    88→        return None
    89→    dims = query.get("dimensions")
    90→    if not isinstance(dims, list):
    91→        return None
    92→    resolved = {dim.strip() for dim in dims if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()}
    93→    return resolved or None
    94→
    95→
    96→def _target_dimensions(args: object) -> set[str] | None:
    97→    """Resolve review target dimensions from CLI args or prepared query state."""
    98→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    99→    if dimensions is not None:
   100→        return dimensions
   101→    if getattr(args, "run_batches", False) or getattr(args, "external_start", False):
   102→        return _prepared_query_dimensions()
   103→    return None
   104→
   105→
   106→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   110→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   111→) -> list[str]:
   112→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   113→    if dimensions is None:
   114→        return scored_dims
   115→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   116→    return sorted(
   117→        dim
   118→        for dim in scored_dims
   119→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   124→    state: StateModel,
   125→    *,
   126→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   127→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   128→    ctx = queue_context(state)
   129→    if not blocking_dims:
   130→        return ctx.snapshot.planned_objective_count, 0
   131→
   132→    normalized_blocking_dims = {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in blocking_dims}
   133→
   134→    def _item_dimension_key(item: dict) -> str | None:
   135→        detail = item.get("detail")
   136→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   137→            return None
   138→        dimension = detail.get("dimension")
   139→        if not isinstance(dimension, str) or not dimension.strip():
   140→            return None
   141→        return _normalize_dimension_key(dimension)
   142→
   143→    subjective_total = sum(
   144→        1
   145→        for item in ctx.snapshot.all_postflight_review_items
   146→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
   147→        if (dim_key := _item_dimension_key(item)) is not None
   148→        and dim_key in normalized_blocking_dims
   149→    )
   150→    return ctx.snapshot.planned_objective_count, subjective_total
   151→
   152→
   153→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   154→    *,
   155→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   156→    objective_total: int,
   157→    subjective_total: int,
   158→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    print(
   161→        colorize(
   162→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   163→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   164→            "red",
   165→        ),
   166→        file=sys.stderr,
   167→    )
   168→    print(
   169→        colorize(
   170→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   171→            "yellow",
   172→        ),
   173→        file=sys.stderr,
   174→    )
   175→    if objective_total > 0:
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Open objective issue(s): {objective_total}",
   179→                "yellow",
   180→            ),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→    if subjective_total > 0:
   184→        print(
   185→            colorize(
   186→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   187→                "yellow",
   188→            ),
   189→            file=sys.stderr,
   190→        )
   191→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   192→    if dimensions is not None:
   193→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   194→        if unscored:
   195→            print(
   196→                colorize(
   197→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   198→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   199→                    "dim",
   200→                ),
   201→                file=sys.stderr,
   202→            )
   203→    print(
   204→        colorize(
   205→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   206→            "dim",
   207→        ),
   208→        file=sys.stderr,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→
   212→def review_rerun_preflight(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    args,
   215→    *,
   216→    state_file=None,
   217→    save_fn=save_state,
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   220→
   221→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   222→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   223→    dimensions and persists state.
   224→    """
   225→    dimensions = _target_dimensions(args)
   226→    normalized_dimensions = (
   227→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   228→        if dimensions is not None
   229→        else None
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   233→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   237→                "yellow",
   238→            )
   239→        )
   240→    else:
   241→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   242→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   243→            state,
   244→            dimensions=dimensions,
   245→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   246→        )
   247→
   248→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   249→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   250→        if blocking_dims:
   251→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   252→                state,
   253→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   254→            )
   255→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   256→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   257→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   258→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   259→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   260→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   261→                )
   262→                raise CommandError(
   263→                    f"rerun blocked: open backlog (objective: {objective_total}, "
   264→                    f"subjective: {subjective_total})",
   265→                    exit_code=1,
   266→                )
   267→
   268→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   269→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   270→    if cleared and state_file:
   271→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   272→        print(
   273→            colorize(
   274→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   275→                "cyan",
   276→            )
   277→        )
   278→
```

```
    64→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    65→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    66→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    67→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    68→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    69→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    70→            continue
    71→        try:
    72→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    73→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    74→            _ = exc
    75→
    76→
    77→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    78→    if lang:
    79→        return
    80→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    84→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    85→    return [
    86→        opts.merge,
    87→        opts.run_batches,
    88→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    89→        opts.external_start,

    96→def _validate_mode_selection(
    97→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    98→    *,
    99→    mode_flags: list[bool],
   100→) -> None:
   101→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   102→        raise CommandError(
   103→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   104→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   105→            exit_code=1,

   107→

   109→        raise CommandError(
   110→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   111→            exit_code=2,
   112→        )
   113→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   114→        raise CommandError(
   115→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   116→            exit_code=2,
   117→        )
   118→
   119→
   120→def _is_default_prepare_mode(opts: ReviewOptions, mode_flags: list[bool]) -> bool:
   121→    """True when the command will run the default prepare flow (no explicit mode)."""
   122→    return not any(mode_flags) and not opts.import_file
   123→
   124→
   125→def _run_review_mode(
   126→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   127→    *,
   128→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   129→    runtime,
   130→    state,
   131→    lang,
   132→    state_file,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if opts.merge:
   135→        do_merge(args)
   136→        return
   137→    if opts.run_batches:
   138→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   139→        do_run_batches(
   140→            args,
   141→            state,
   142→            lang,
   143→            state_file,
   144→            config=runtime.config,
   145→        )
   146→        return
   147→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   148→        do_import_run(
   149→            opts.import_run_dir,
   150→            state,

   152→            state_file,
   153→            config=runtime.config,
   154→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   155→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   156→            scan_path=opts.path,
   157→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   158→        )
   159→        return
   160→    if opts.external_start:
   161→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   162→        do_external_start(
   163→            args,
   164→            state,
   165→            lang,
   166→            config=runtime.config,
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→    if opts.external_submit:
   170→        do_external_submit(
   171→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   172→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   173→            state=state,
   174→            lang=lang,
   175→            state_file=state_file,
   176→            config=runtime.config,
   177→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   178→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   179→            scan_path=opts.path,
   180→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   181→        )
   182→        return
   183→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   184→        do_validate_import(
   185→            opts.validate_import_file,
   186→            lang,
   187→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   188→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   189→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   190→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   191→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   192→            ),
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→
   196→    if opts.import_file:
   197→        do_import(
   198→            opts.import_file,
   199→            state,
   200→            lang,
   201→            state_file,
   202→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   203→                config=runtime.config,
   204→                allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   205→                manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   206→                attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   207→                manual_attest=opts.attest,
   208→            ),
   209→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   210→        )
   211→        return
   212→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   213→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   214→
   215→
   216→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   217→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   218→    _enable_live_review_output()
   219→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   220→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   221→    state = runtime.state
   222→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   223→    _require_lang(lang)
   224→
   225→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   226→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   227→    _validate_mode_selection(
   228→        opts,
   229→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # Default prepare and --run-batches need real scan-backed context rather
   233→    # than reconstructed plan inventory.
   234→    if _is_default_prepare_mode(opts, mode_flags) or opts.run_batches:
   235→        if not require_scan_metrics(state):
   236→            return
   237→
   238→    _run_review_mode(
   239→        args,
   240→        opts=opts,
   241→        runtime=runtime,
   242→        state=state,
   243→        lang=lang,
   244→        state_file=state_file,
   245→    )
   246→
```

```
   236→def _build_prepared_packet_contract(args, *, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, object]:
   237→    """Build normalized invocation contract for prepared packet reuse."""
   238→    return prepared_packet_contract(resolve_review_packet_context(args), config=config)
   239→
   240→
   241→def _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   242→    packet: dict[str, object],
   243→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   244→) -> str | None:
   245→    """Return mismatch reason for prepared packet reuse, else ``None``."""
   246→    raw_contract = packet.get(_PREPARED_PACKET_CONTRACT_KEY)
   247→    if not isinstance(raw_contract, dict):
   248→        return "missing prepared packet contract metadata"
   249→
   250→    for key in (
   251→        "path",
   252→        "dimensions",
   253→        "retrospective",
   254→        "retrospective_max_issues",
   255→        "retrospective_max_batch_items",
   256→        "config_hash",
   257→    ):
   258→        if raw_contract.get(key) != expected_contract.get(key):
   259→            return f"contract field '{key}' differs"
   260→    return None
   261→
   262→
   263→def _try_load_prepared_packet(
   264→    *,
   265→    expected_contract: dict[str, object],
   266→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
   267→    """Load prepared packet from query.json when shape and contract match."""
   268→    try:
   269→        qf = query_file_path()
   270→        if not qf.exists():
   271→            return None, None
   272→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
   273→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError):
   274→        return None, "query.json is missing or invalid JSON"
   275→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   276→        return None, "prepared packet payload is not an object"
   277→    if "investigation_batches" not in data:
   278→        return None, "prepared packet is missing investigation batches"
   279→    batches = data["investigation_batches"]
   280→    if not isinstance(batches, list) or not batches:
   281→        return None, "prepared packet has no investigation batches"
   282→
   283→    mismatch_reason = _prepared_packet_contract_mismatch_reason(
   284→        data,
   285→        expected_contract,
   286→    )
   287→    if mismatch_reason is not None:
   288→        return None, mismatch_reason
   289→    return data, None
   290→
   291→
   292→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
   293→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   294→    normalized_results: list[BatchResultPayload] = []
   295→    for result in batch_results:
   296→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
   297→            payload = result.to_dict()
   298→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
   299→                normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, payload))
   300→                continue
   301→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   302→            normalized_results.append(cast(BatchResultPayload, result))
   303→    return merge_batch_results(
   304→        normalized_results,
   305→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   306→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
   307→    )
   308→
   309→
   310→def _load_or_prepare_packet(
   311→    args,
   312→    *,
   313→    state: dict,
   314→    lang,
   315→    config: dict,
   316→    stamp: str,
   317→) -> tuple[dict, Path, Path]:
   318→    """Load packet override or prepare a fresh packet snapshot."""
   319→    packet_override = getattr(args, "packet", None)
   320→    if packet_override:
   321→        packet_path = Path(packet_override)
   322→        if not packet_path.exists():
   323→            raise PacketValidationError(f"packet not found: {packet_override}", exit_code=1)
   324→        try:
   325→            packet = json.loads(packet_path.read_text())
   326→        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   327→            raise PacketValidationError(f"reading packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   328→        blind_path = _blind_packet_path()
   329→        blind_packet = build_blind_packet(packet)
   330→        safe_write_text(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
   331→        print(colorize(f"  Immutable packet: {packet_path}", "dim"))
   332→        print(colorize(f"  Blind packet: {blind_path}", "dim"))
   333→        return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   334→
   335→    # When no explicit --packet and no explicit --dimensions were given,
   336→    # check whether a prior ``review --prepare`` left a valid query.json
   337→    # packet we can reuse instead of rebuilding from scratch.
   338→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   339→
   340→    # Validate explicit dimensions against the language's scored dimensions.
   341→    if dims:
   342→        lang_obj = lang
   343→        lang_name = getattr(lang_obj, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang_obj, "lang", ""))
   344→        if lang_name:
   345→            valid_dims = set(scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name))
   346→            if valid_dims:
   347→                invalid = sorted(dims - valid_dims)
   348→                if invalid:
   349→                    valid_list = ", ".join(sorted(valid_dims))
```

```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective issues and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import load_query_result
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    17→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel, save_state
    18→
    19→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    20→
    21→
    22→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    23→    state: StateModel,
    24→    *,
    25→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    26→) -> list[str]:
    27→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    28→
    29→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    30→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    31→
    32→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    33→    """
    34→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    35→    cleared: list[str] = []
    36→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    37→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    38→            continue
    39→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    40→            continue
    41→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    42→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    43→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    44→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    45→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    46→    return cleared
    47→
    48→
    49→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    50→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    51→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    52→    scored: list[str] = []
    53→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    54→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    55→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    56→                scored.append(dim_key)
    57→        elif isinstance(assessment, int | float) and assessment:
    58→            scored.append(dim_key)
    59→    return sorted(scored)
    60→
    61→
    62→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    63→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    64→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    65→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    66→
    67→
    68→def _prepared_query_dimensions() -> set[str] | None:
    69→    """Return prepared review dimensions from query.json when available."""
    70→    query_result = load_query_result()
    71→    if not query_result.ok:
    72→        if query_result.error_kind in {"query_parse_error", "query_invalid_shape"}:
    73→            detail = query_result.message or query_result.error_kind
    74→            print(
    75→                colorize(
    76→                    f"  Warning: ignoring invalid query artifact ({detail}).",
    77→                    "yellow",
    78→                ),
    79→                file=sys.stderr,
    80→            )
    81→        return None
    82→    query = query_result.payload or {}
    83→    if not isinstance(query, dict):
    84→        return None
    85→    if query.get("command") != "review" or query.get("mode") != "holistic":
    86→        return None
    87→    if not isinstance(query.get("investigation_batches"), list):
    88→        return None
    89→    dims = query.get("dimensions")
    90→    if not isinstance(dims, list):
    91→        return None
    92→    resolved = {dim.strip() for dim in dims if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()}
    93→    return resolved or None
    94→
    95→
    96→def _target_dimensions(args: object) -> set[str] | None:
    97→    """Resolve review target dimensions from CLI args or prepared query state."""
    98→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
    99→    if dimensions is not None:
   100→        return dimensions
   101→    if getattr(args, "run_batches", False) or getattr(args, "external_start", False):
   102→        return _prepared_query_dimensions()
   103→    return None
   104→
   105→
   106→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   107→    state: StateModel,
   108→    *,
   109→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   110→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   111→) -> list[str]:
   112→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   113→    if dimensions is None:
   114→        return scored_dims
   115→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   116→    return sorted(
   117→        dim
   118→        for dim in scored_dims
   119→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   124→    state: StateModel,
   125→    *,
   126→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   127→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   128→    ctx = queue_context(state)
   129→    if not blocking_dims:
   130→        return ctx.snapshot.planned_objective_count, 0
   131→
   132→    normalized_blocking_dims = {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in blocking_dims}
   133→
   134→    def _item_dimension_key(item: dict) -> str | None:
   135→        detail = item.get("detail")
   136→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   137→            return None
   138→        dimension = detail.get("dimension")
   139→        if not isinstance(dimension, str) or not dimension.strip():
   140→            return None
   141→        return _normalize_dimension_key(dimension)
   142→
   143→    subjective_total = sum(
   144→        1
   145→        for item in ctx.snapshot.all_postflight_review_items
   146→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
   147→        if (dim_key := _item_dimension_key(item)) is not None
   148→        and dim_key in normalized_blocking_dims
   149→    )
   150→    return ctx.snapshot.planned_objective_count, subjective_total
   151→
   152→
   153→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   154→    *,
   155→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   156→    objective_total: int,
   157→    subjective_total: int,
   158→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   159→) -> None:
   160→    print(
   161→        colorize(
   162→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   163→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   164→            "red",
   165→        ),
   166→        file=sys.stderr,
   167→    )
   168→    print(
   169→        colorize(
   170→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   171→            "yellow",
   172→        ),
   173→        file=sys.stderr,
   174→    )
   175→    if objective_total > 0:
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Open objective issue(s): {objective_total}",
   179→                "yellow",
   180→            ),
   181→            file=sys.stderr,
   182→        )
   183→    if subjective_total > 0:
   184→        print(
   185→            colorize(
   186→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   187→                "yellow",
   188→            ),
   189→            file=sys.stderr,
   190→        )
   191→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   192→    if dimensions is not None:
   193→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   194→        if unscored:
   195→            print(
   196→                colorize(
   197→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   198→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   199→                    "dim",
   200→                ),
   201→                file=sys.stderr,
   202→            )
   203→    print(
   204→        colorize(
   205→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   206→            "dim",
   207→        ),
   208→        file=sys.stderr,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→
   212→def review_rerun_preflight(
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    args,
   215→    *,
   216→    state_file=None,
   217→    save_fn=save_state,
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   220→
   221→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   222→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   223→    dimensions and persists state.
   224→    """
   225→    dimensions = _target_dimensions(args)
   226→    normalized_dimensions = (
   227→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   228→        if dimensions is not None
   229→        else None
   230→    )
   231→
   232→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   233→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   237→                "yellow",
   238→            )
   239→        )
   240→    else:
   241→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   242→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   243→            state,
   244→            dimensions=dimensions,
   245→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   246→        )
   247→
   248→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   249→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   250→        if blocking_dims:
   251→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   252→                state,
   253→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   254→            )
   255→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   256→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   257→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   258→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   259→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   260→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   261→                )
   262→                raise CommandError(
   263→                    f"rerun blocked: open backlog (objective: {objective_total}, "
   264→                    f"subjective: {subjective_total})",
   265→                    exit_code=1,
   266→                )
   267→
   268→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   269→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   270→    if cleared and state_file:
   271→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   272→        print(
   273→            colorize(
   274→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   275→                "cyan",
   276→            )
   277→        )
   278→
```

```
    55→def _resolve_import_payload(
    56→    import_file,
    57→    *,
    58→    lang_name: str,
    59→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,
    60→) -> tuple[dict, bool, str | None]:
    61→    """Validate import flags and load payload with policy checks."""
    62→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
    63→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
    64→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
    65→    )
    66→    try:
    67→        validate_import_flag_combos(
    68→            attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
    69→            allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
    70→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
    71→            override_attest=override_attest,
    72→        )
    73→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
    74→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
    75→
    76→    try:
    77→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
    78→            import_file,
    79→            config=build_import_load_config(
    80→                lang_name=lang_name,
    81→                import_config=import_config,
    82→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
    83→                override_attest=override_attest,
    84→            ),
    85→        )
    86→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
    87→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
    88→            exc.errors,
    89→            import_file=str(import_file),
    90→            colorize_fn=colorize,
    91→        )
    92→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
    93→
    94→    return issues_data, override_enabled, override_attest
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_working_state(state: dict, state_file) -> dict:
    98→    """Return state snapshot used for import mutation/dry-run rendering."""
    99→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   100→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   101→        return copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   102→    return copy.deepcopy(state)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _apply_assessment_policy(
   106→    *,
   107→    working_state: dict,
   108→    issues_data: dict,
   109→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   110→) -> int:
   111→    """Apply provisional/clear flags based on assessment policy mode."""
   112→    assessment_keys = imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   113→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   114→        return mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   115→            working_state,
   116→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   117→        )
   118→    if assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   119→        clear_provisional_override_flags(
   120→            working_state,
   121→            assessment_keys=assessment_keys,
   122→        )
   123→    return 0
   124→
   125→
   126→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   127→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   128→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   129→        return
   130→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   131→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   132→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   133→        details_lines.append(
   134→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   135→        )
   136→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   137→    if details_lines:
   138→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   139→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   140→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   141→
   142→
   143→def _append_assessment_import_audit(
   144→    *,
   145→    working_state: dict,
   146→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   147→    provisional_count: int,
   148→    override_attest: str | None,
   149→    import_file,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Record audit metadata for assessment-bearing import payloads."""
   152→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   153→        return
   154→    audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   155→    audit.append(
   156→        {
   157→            "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   158→            "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   159→            "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   160→            "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   161→            "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   162→            "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   163→            "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   164→            "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   165→            "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   166→            "import_file": str(import_file),
   167→        }
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _persist_import_state(
   172→    *,
   173→    state: dict,
   174→    working_state: dict,
   175→    state_file,
   176→    diff: dict,
   177→    assessment_mode: str,
   178→    config: dict | None,
   179→    import_file: str,
   180→    import_payload: dict,
   181→) -> None:
   182→    """Persist imported state and synchronize the work plan."""
   183→    state.clear()
   184→    state.update(working_state)
   185→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   186→    sync_plan_after_import(
   187→        state,
   188→        diff,
   189→        assessment_mode,
   190→        state_file=state_file,
   191→        config=config,
   192→        import_file=import_file,
   193→        import_payload=import_payload,
   194→    )
   195→
   196→
   197→def _guard_pending_import_scores_match(
   198→    *,
   199→    state: dict,
   200→    state_file,
   201→    import_file: str,
   202→    issues_data: dict,
   203→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
   204→) -> None:
   205→    """Refuse durable imports that do not match the queued score-import batch."""
   206→    if assessment_policy.mode not in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   207→        return
   208→    plan_path = plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
   209→    if not has_living_plan(plan_path):
   210→        return
   211→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   212→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   213→        return
   214→    pending_meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   215→    matches, reason = import_scores_meta_matches(
   216→        pending_meta,
   217→        import_file=import_file,
   218→        import_payload=issues_data,
   219→    )
   220→    if matches:
   221→        return
   222→    expected_file = ""
   223→    if isinstance(pending_meta, dict):
   224→        expected_file = str(pending_meta.get("import_file", "")).strip()
   225→    raise CommandError(
   226→        "Refusing durable score import: the pending "
   227→        "`workflow::import-scores` task is bound to a different review batch.\n"
   228→        f"  - {reason}\n"
   229→        + (f"Expected queued import file: {expected_file}\n" if expected_file else "")
   230→        + "Use the exact file shown by `desloppify next`, or clear the stale workflow item first.",
   231→        exit_code=1,
   232→    )
   233→

   244→
   245→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores")
   246→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
   247→        return False
```

### **Key Finding for Issue #395**
The problematic output is in **`queue_flow.py` at line 243**, where the user message says:

```
    47→        default=30,
    48→        minimum=1,
    49→    )

    51→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    52→        default=20,
    53→        minimum=1,
    54→    )
    55→    return ReviewPacketContext(
    56→        path=Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    57→        dimensions=dimensions,
    58→        retrospective=retrospective,
    59→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    60→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
```

```
481→def do_import_run(
   482→    run_dir_path: str,
   483→    state: dict,
   484→    lang,
   485→    state_file: str,
   486→    *,
   487→    config: dict | None = None,
   488→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   489→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   490→    scan_path: str = ".",
   491→    dry_run: bool = False,
   492→) -> None:
   493→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   494→
   495→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   496→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   497→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   498→    """
   499→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   500→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   501→
   502→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   503→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   504→    selected = summary.get("selected_batches", [])
   505→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   506→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   507→
   508→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   509→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   510→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in selected]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   511→    output_files = {
   512→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   513→        for idx in selected_indexes
   514→    }
   515→
   516→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   517→    if missing:
   518→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   519→
   520→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   521→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   522→        failures=[],
   523→        output_files=output_files,
   524→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   525→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   526→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: normalize_batch_result(
   527→            payload,
   528→            dims,
   529→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   530→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   531→        ),
   532→    )
   533→
   534→    if not batch_results:
   535→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   536→
   537→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   538→    if failures:
   539→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   540→
   541→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   542→
   543→    # Reuse the canonical merge+metadata boundary from normal batch execution.
   544→    raw_batches = packet.get("investigation_batches", [])
   545→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   546→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   547→        raw_batches if isinstance(raw_batches, list) else [],
   548→        dimension_prompts=raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else None,
   549→    )
   550→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   551→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   552→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name) if lang_name else []
   553→    merged_path, missing_after_import = _merge_and_write_results(
   554→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   555→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   556→        batch_results=batch_results,
   557→        batches=batches,
   558→        successful_indexes=successful_indexes,
   559→        packet=packet,
   560→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   561→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   562→        scan_path=scan_path,
   563→        runner=runner,
   564→        prompt_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   565→        stamp=stamp,
   566→        run_dir=run_dir,
   567→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   568→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   569→    )
   570→    _enforce_import_coverage(
   571→        missing_after_import=missing_after_import,
   572→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   573→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   574→        scan_path=scan_path,
   575→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   576→    )
   577→
   578→    # -- import with trusted source --
   579→    _do_import(
   580→        str(merged_path),
   581→        state,
   582→        lang,
   583→        state_file,
   584→        import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   585→            config=config,
   586→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   587→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   588→            trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   589→        ),
   590→        dry_run=dry_run,
   591→    )
   592→
   593→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   594→    if scan_after_import and not dry_run:
   595→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   596→        if lang_name:
   597→            run_followup_scan(

   599→                scan_path=scan_path,
```

```
413→def do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   414→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   415→    from ..runtime.policy import resolve_batch_run_policy  # noqa: PLC0415
   416→
   417→    project_root = _runtime_project_root()
   418→    subagent_runs_dir = _subagent_runs_dir()
   419→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   420→    batch_deps = _build_batch_run_deps(
   421→        policy=policy,
   422→        project_root=project_root,
   423→    )
   424→    prepared = review_batch_phases_mod.prepare_batch_run(
   425→        args=args,
   426→        state=state,
   427→        lang=lang,
   428→        config=config or {},
   429→        deps=batch_deps,
   430→        project_root=project_root,
   431→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
   432→    )
   433→    if prepared is None:
   434→        return
   435→
   436→    executed = review_batch_phases_mod.execute_batch_run(
   437→        prepared=prepared,
   438→        deps=batch_deps,
   439→    )
   440→    review_batch_phases_mod.merge_and_import_batch_run(
   441→        prepared=prepared,
   442→        executed=executed,
   443→        state_file=state_file,
   444→        deps=batch_deps,
   445→    )
   446→
   447→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   448→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   449→
   450→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   451→    """
   452→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   453→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   454→
   455→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   456→    if not summary_path.exists():
   457→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   458→    try:
   459→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   460→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   461→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   462→
   463→    selected = summary.get("selected_batches", [])
   464→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   465→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   466→
   467→    if not selected:
   468→        raise CommandError("no selected batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   469→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   470→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   471→
   472→    try:
   473→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   474→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   475→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   476→
   477→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   478→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   479→
   480→

   482→    run_dir_path: str,
   483→    state: dict,
   484→    lang,
   485→    state_file: str,
   486→    *,
   487→    config: dict | None = None,
```

```
1→"""Result reconciliation, merge writing, and import-finalization helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from pathlib import Path

     8→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError

    13→    collect_reviewed_files_from_batches,
    14→    enforce_trusted_import_coverage_gate,

    21→def collect_and_reconcile_results(
    22→    *,
    23→    collect_batch_results_fn,
    24→    selected_indexes: list[int],
    25→    execution_failures: list[int],
    26→    output_files: dict,
    27→    packet: dict,
    28→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
    29→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
    30→    colorize_fn=None,
    31→) -> tuple[list[dict], list[int], list[int], set[int]]:
    32→    """Collect batch results and reconcile per-batch status entries."""
    33→    allowed_dims = {
    34→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
    35→    }
    36→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
    37→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
    38→        failures=execution_failures,
    39→        output_files=output_files,
    40→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
    41→    )
    42→
    43→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
    44→    failure_set = set(failures)
    45→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
    46→    for idx in selected_indexes:
    47→        key = str(idx + 1)
    48→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
    49→            key,
    50→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
    51→        )
    52→        if idx not in failure_set:
    53→            # Batch succeeded — distinguish recovered (execution failed but payload valid)
    54→            # from clean success.
    55→            if idx in execution_failure_set:
    56→                state["status"] = "recovered"
    57→            else:
    58→                state["status"] = "succeeded"
    59→            continue
    60→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
    61→            state["status"] = "failed"
    62→            continue
    63→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
    64→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
    65→            continue
    66→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
    67→
    68→    recovered = sorted(
    69→        idx + 1
    70→        for idx in selected_indexes
    71→        if idx in execution_failure_set and idx not in failure_set
    72→    )
    73→    if recovered and colorize_fn is not None:
    74→        print(
    75→            colorize_fn(
    76→                f"  Recovered batches (execution exited non-zero but payload valid): {recovered}",
    77→                "green",
    78→            )
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    return batch_results, successful_indexes, failures, failure_set
    82→
    83→
    84→def merge_and_write_results(
    85→    *,
    86→    merge_batch_results_fn,
    87→    build_import_provenance_fn,
    88→    batch_results: list[dict],
    89→    batches: list,
    90→    successful_indexes: list[int],
    91→    packet: dict,
    92→    packet_dimensions: list[str],
    93→    scored_dimensions: list[str],
    94→    scan_path: str,
    95→    runner: str,
    96→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
    97→    stamp: str,
    98→    run_dir: Path,
    99→    safe_write_text_fn,
   100→    colorize_fn,
   101→) -> tuple[Path, list[str]]:
   102→    """Merge batch results, enrich with metadata, and write to disk."""
   103→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   104→    quality = merged.get("review_quality", {})
   105→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   106→        batches=batches,
   107→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   108→    )
   109→    full_sweep_included = any(
   110→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   111→        for idx in successful_indexes
   112→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   113→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   114→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   115→    )
   116→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   117→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   118→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   119→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   120→    }
   121→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   122→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   123→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   124→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   125→    if reviewed_files:
   126→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   127→        print(
   128→            colorize_fn(
   129→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   130→                "dim",
   131→            )
   132→        )
   133→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   134→        runner=runner,
   135→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   136→        run_stamp=stamp,
   137→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   138→    )
   139→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   140→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   141→    )
   142→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   143→        [
   144→            issue.get("dimension")
   145→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])
   146→            if isinstance(issue, dict)
   147→        ]
   148→    )
   149→    merged_imported_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   150→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   151→    )
   152→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   153→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   154→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   155→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   156→        scan_path=scan_path,
   157→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   158→    )
   159→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   160→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   161→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   162→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   163→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   164→    }
   165→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   166→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   167→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   168→    print_review_quality(quality, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   169→    return merged_path, missing_after_import
   170→
   171→
   172→def import_and_finalize(
   173→    *,
   174→    do_import_fn,
   175→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   176→    merged_path: Path,
   177→    state,
   178→    lang,
   179→    state_file,
   180→    config: dict,
   181→    allow_partial: bool,
   182→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   183→    failure_set: set[int],
   184→    append_run_log,
   185→    args,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Import merged results and optionally run a follow-up scan."""
   188→    try:
   189→        do_import_fn(
   190→            str(merged_path),
   191→            state,
   192→            lang,
   193→            state_file,
   194→            import_config=ReviewImportConfig(
   195→                config=config,
   196→                allow_partial=allow_partial,
   197→                trusted_assessment_source=True,
   198→                trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   199→            ),
   200→        )
   201→    except SystemExit as exc:
   202→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   203→        raise
   204→    except Exception as exc:
   205→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   206→        raise
   207→    append_run_log(
   208→        "run-finished "
   209→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   210→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   211→    )
   212→
   213→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   214→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   215→            lang_name=lang.name,
   216→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   217→        )
   218→        if followup_code != 0:
   219→            raise CommandError(
   220→                f"Error: follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   221→                exit_code=followup_code,
   222→            )
   223→
   224→
   225→def enforce_import_coverage(
   226→    *,
   227→    missing_after_import: list[str],
   228→    packet_dimensions: list[str],
   229→    allow_partial: bool,
   230→    scan_path: str,
   231→    colorize_fn,
   232→) -> None:
   233→    """Apply trusted import coverage gate after merge output is written."""
   234→    enforce_trusted_import_coverage_gate(
   235→        missing_dims=missing_after_import,
   236→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   237→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   238→        scan_path=scan_path,
   239→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   240→    )
   241→
```
